-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT n, builtin, rule IS NOT NULL, parent, name FROM folders
    SCAN folders

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT n, id, name, person, frecency, opened_at, last_at, community FROM chats
    SCAN chats

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT n, name, push_name, verified_name FROM people
    SCAN people

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT coalesce(max(seq), 0) FROM events
    SEARCH events

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT person, jid FROM person_jids WHERE jid LIKE '%@s.whatsapp.net'
    SCAN person_jids

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT folder, chat, person FROM folder_items
    SCAN folder_items

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT type, key FROM events WHERE seq > ?1 AND seq <= ?2 AND type IN ('chat', 'person', 'folder') ORDER BY seq
    SEARCH events USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid>? AND rowid<?)

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT n, id, name, person, frecency, opened_at, last_at, community FROM chats WHERE n = ?1
    SEARCH chats USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = ?1 UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = ?2
    COMPOUND QUERY
      LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
        SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
      UNION ALL
        SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT n, name, push_name, verified_name FROM people WHERE n = ?1
    SEARCH people USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT jid FROM person_jids WHERE person = ?1 ORDER BY n
    SEARCH person_jids USING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)

-- src/find/index.rs
SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = ?1
    SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)

-- src/media/cache.rs
SELECT bytes, files FROM media_cache WHERE id = 1
    SEARCH media_cache USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/media/cache.rs
SELECT path, small, large, thumbnail FROM media_files WHERE sha256 = ?1
    SEARCH media_files USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?)

-- src/media/cache.rs
SELECT sha256 FROM media_drop WHERE sha256 > ?1 ORDER BY sha256 LIMIT ?2
    SEARCH media_drop USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256>?)

-- src/media/cache.rs
SELECT bytes FROM media_cache WHERE id = 1
    SEARCH media_cache USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/media/cache.rs
SELECT used_at, sha256, stored FROM media_files WHERE (used_at, sha256) > (?1, ?2) ORDER BY used_at, sha256 LIMIT ?3
    SEARCH media_files USING INDEX media_files_by_use ((used_at,sha256)>(?,?))

-- src/media/fetch.rs
SELECT envelope, raw FROM messages WHERE n = ?1
    SEARCH messages USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/media/fetch.rs
SELECT message, variant, retried_at FROM media WHERE state = 'waiting_for_phone' ORDER BY retried_at LIMIT 64
    SCAN media USING COVERING INDEX media_waiting

-- src/media/mod.rs
SELECT settled, made FROM media_made WHERE id = 1
    SEARCH media_made USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/media/mod.rs
SELECT made - settled, 0 FROM media_made WHERE id = 1
    SEARCH media_made USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/media/mod.rs
SELECT m.kind, md.seconds FROM messages m LEFT JOIN media md ON md.message = m.n AND md.variant = 'sd' WHERE m.n = ?1
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/mod.rs
SELECT m.n, m.deleted, m.view_once, md.sha256, f.path, md.name, md.variant FROM chats c JOIN messages m ON m.chat = c.n AND m.id = ?2 LEFT JOIN media md ON md.message = m.n AND md.variant = ?3 LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE c.id = ?1
    SEARCH c USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1 (id=?)
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_id (chat=? AND id=?)
    SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/mod.rs
SELECT m.n, m.deleted, m.view_once, md.sha256, f.path, md.name, md.variant FROM chats c CROSS JOIN messages m ON m.chat = c.n AND m.id = ?1 LEFT JOIN media md ON md.message = m.n AND md.variant = ?2 LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 LIMIT 2
    SCAN c USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_id (chat=? AND id=?)
    SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/mod.rs
SELECT 1 FROM backfills WHERE name = ?1 AND after < ?2 AND upto >= ?2
    SEARCH backfills USING PRIMARY KEY (name=?)

-- src/media/previews.rs
SELECT raw FROM messages WHERE n = ?1
    SEARCH messages USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/media/save.rs
SELECT f.path, f.small, f.large, md.state, md.error, md.wanted IS NOT NULL FROM media md LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.message = ?1 AND md.variant = 'sd'
    SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?)
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/save.rs
SELECT f.path, md.state, md.error, md.wanted IS NOT NULL FROM media md LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.message = ?1 AND md.variant = ?2
    SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?)
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/schedule.rs
SELECT md.message, md.variant, md.keys, md.mime, md.bytes, md.name, md.sha256, md.enc_sha256, md.media_key, md.direct_path, md.thumbnail, md.state, md.asked, md.wanted, m.kind, m.view_once, m.envelope IS NOT NULL, f.path, f.small IS NOT NULL AND f.large IS NOT NULL, f.thumbnail IS NOT NULL, md.cancelled, md.arrived FROM media md JOIN messages m ON m.n = md.message LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.asked = 1 AND md.wanted IS NOT NULL ORDER BY md.wanted LIMIT ?1
    SEARCH md USING INDEX media_asked (wanted>?)
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/schedule.rs
SELECT md.message, md.variant, md.keys, md.mime, md.bytes, md.name, md.sha256, md.enc_sha256, md.media_key, md.direct_path, md.thumbnail, md.state, md.asked, md.wanted, m.kind, m.view_once, m.envelope IS NOT NULL, f.path, f.small IS NOT NULL AND f.large IS NOT NULL, f.thumbnail IS NOT NULL, md.cancelled, md.arrived FROM media md JOIN messages m ON m.n = md.message LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.arrived = 1 AND md.wanted IS NOT NULL AND md.asked = 0 ORDER BY md.wanted LIMIT ?1
    SEARCH md USING INDEX media_arrived (wanted>?)
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/schedule.rs
SELECT md.message, md.variant, md.keys, md.mime, md.bytes, md.name, md.sha256, md.enc_sha256, md.media_key, md.direct_path, md.thumbnail, md.state, md.asked, md.wanted, m.kind, m.view_once, m.envelope IS NOT NULL, f.path, f.small IS NOT NULL AND f.large IS NOT NULL, f.thumbnail IS NOT NULL, md.cancelled, md.arrived FROM media md JOIN messages m ON m.n = md.message LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.wanted IS NOT NULL AND md.asked = 0 AND md.arrived = 0 ORDER BY md.wanted DESC LIMIT ?1
    SEARCH md USING INDEX media_wanted (wanted>?)
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/media/settle.rs
SELECT f.sha256, f.made, f.small, f.large, f.thumbnail, (SELECT md.message FROM media md WHERE md.sha256 = f.sha256 AND md.variant = 'sd' LIMIT 1) FROM media_files f WHERE f.made > ?1 AND f.made <= ?2 ORDER BY f.made LIMIT ?3
    SEARCH f USING INDEX media_files_by_made (made>? AND made<?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH md USING COVERING INDEX media_by_hash (sha256=?)

-- src/media/settle.rs
SELECT f.sha256, f.made, f.small, f.large, f.thumbnail, (SELECT md.message FROM media md WHERE md.sha256 = f.sha256 AND md.variant = 'sd' LIMIT 1) FROM media_files f WHERE f.sha256 = ?1
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH md USING COVERING INDEX media_by_hash (sha256=?)

-- src/media/settle.rs
SELECT f.sha256, f.made, f.small, f.large, f.thumbnail, (SELECT md.message FROM media md WHERE md.sha256 = f.sha256 AND md.variant = 'sd' LIMIT 1) FROM media_files f WHERE f.sha256 > ?1 ORDER BY f.sha256 LIMIT ?2
    SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256>?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH md USING COVERING INDEX media_by_hash (sha256=?)

-- src/media/settle.rs
SELECT settled, made FROM media_made WHERE id = 1
    SEARCH media_made USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/organise.rs

-- src/presence.rs

    SEARCH person_jids USING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)

-- src/presence.rs
SELECT p.person FROM favourites s CROSS JOIN folder_people p ON p.folder = s.folder UNION ALL SELECT c.person FROM favourites s CROSS JOIN chats c ON c.n = s.chat WHERE c.person IS NOT NULL
    COMPOUND QUERY
      LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
        SCAN s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1
        SEARCH p USING COVERING INDEX folder_people_by_sort (folder=?)
      UNION ALL
        SCAN s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2
        SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/presence.rs
SELECT n, about_at FROM people WHERE (name IS NOT NULL OR direct_chats > 0) AND (about_at IS NULL OR about_at < ?1) ORDER BY about_at LIMIT ?2
    SCAN people USING INDEX people_about_due

-- src/presence.rs
SELECT min(about_at) FROM people WHERE (name IS NOT NULL OR direct_chats > 0) AND about_at >= ?1
    SEARCH people USING INDEX people_about_due (about_at>?)

-- src/send/actions.rs
SELECT coalesce(k.id, c.id), c.kind, m.mine, j.jid, m.at, m.kind, m.deleted, m.status, m.n, coalesce(k.n, c.n), m.view_once, m.forwarded, m.raw, m.text, m.edited_at IS NOT NULL, EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM outbox a WHERE a.target = m.n AND a.kind = 'revoke' AND a.state <> 'failed') FROM chats c JOIN messages m ON m.chat = c.n AND m.id = ?2 LEFT JOIN person_jids j ON j.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN chat_merges cm ON cm.gone = c.n LEFT JOIN chats k ON k.n = cm.keep WHERE c.id = ?1
    SEARCH c USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1 (id=?)
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_id (chat=? AND id=?)
    SEARCH j USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH cm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH k USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH a USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)

-- src/send/actions.rs
SELECT coalesce(k.id, c.id), c.kind, m.mine, j.jid, m.at, m.kind, m.deleted, m.status, m.n, coalesce(k.n, c.n), m.view_once, m.forwarded, m.raw, m.text, m.edited_at IS NOT NULL, EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM outbox a WHERE a.target = m.n AND a.kind = 'revoke' AND a.state <> 'failed') FROM chats c CROSS JOIN messages m ON m.chat = c.n AND m.id = ?1 LEFT JOIN person_jids j ON j.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN chat_merges cm ON cm.gone = c.n LEFT JOIN chats k ON k.n = cm.keep LIMIT 4
    SCAN c
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_id (chat=? AND id=?)
    SEARCH j USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH cm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH k USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH a USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)

-- src/send/once.rs
SELECT m.n, c.id, j.jid, m.mine, m.view_once, m.deleted, m.kind, m.raw, m.detail, EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM media md WHERE md.message = m.n AND md.variant = 'sd' AND md.sha256 IS NOT NULL) FROM chats c JOIN messages m ON m.chat = c.n AND m.id = ?2 LEFT JOIN person_jids j ON j.n = m.sender WHERE c.id = ?1
    SEARCH c USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1 (id=?)
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_id (chat=? AND id=?)
    SEARCH j USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?)

-- src/send/once.rs

    SCAN c USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_id (chat=? AND id=?)

SELECT m.n, m.id, m.chat, c.id, m.v, m.at, m.t, m.mine, m.kind, m.shelf, m.text, coalesce(m.summary, m.text, ''), m.detail, m.view_once, p.n, p.name, p.push_name, p.verified_name, j.jid, (SELECT json_object('state', iif(f.path IS NOT NULL, 'local', md.state), 'progress', md.progress, 'error', md.error, 'path', f.path, 'small', f.small, 'large', f.large, 'thumbnail', f.thumbnail, 'has_thumbnail', md.thumbnail, 'sha256', hex(md.sha256), 'hd', (SELECT json_object('path', hf.path) FROM media h LEFT JOIN media_files hf ON hf.sha256 = h.sha256 WHERE h.message = m.n AND h.variant = 'hd')) FROM media md LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.message = m.n AND md.variant = 'sd') FROM messages m JOIN chats c ON c.n = m.chat LEFT JOIN person_jids j ON j.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = j.person WHERE m.n = ?1
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH j USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      SEARCH md USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?)
      SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
        SEARCH h USING PRIMARY KEY (message=? AND variant=?)
        SEARCH hf USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

    SEARCH c USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1 (id=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN

      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)
      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 10
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_communities (sort<?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)

    SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_sort (folder=? AND (sort,chat)<(?,?))
    SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN

    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN

      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)

p.verified_name, m.n, m.at, m.mine, m.kind, m.status, coalesce(m.summary, m.text, ''), mp.name, mp.push_name, mp.verified_name, mj.jid, iif(c.name IS NULL AND c.kind <> 'direct', (SELECT group_concat(shown, ', ') FROM ( SELECT coalesce(gp.name, gp.push_name, gp.verified_name) AS shown FROM group_members g JOIN person_jids gj ON gj.n = g.member JOIN people gp ON gp.n = gj.person WHERE g.chat = c.n AND shown IS NOT NULL LIMIT 4)), NULL), m.edited_at, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), (SELECT json_group_array(folder) FROM (SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = c.n UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM = iif(c.kind = 'community', coalesce((SELECT g.last_message FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id AND g.last_message IS NOT NULL ORDER BY g.last_at DESC LIMIT 1), c.last_message), c.last_message) LEFT JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE c.archived = 0 AND (c.sort, c.n) < (?2, ?3) AND (?5 = 0 OR c.unread > 0 OR c.marked_unread <> 0) AND (?6 = 0 OR c.mentions > 0) AND (?7 IS NULL OR iif(?7 = 'direct', c.kind = 'direct', iif(?7 = 'group', c.kind IN ('group', 'announcement'), c.kind = 'community' OR c.community IS NOT NULL))) AND (?8 IS NULL OR c.community = ?8 OR c.id = ?8) AND (?9 = 0 OR c.muted_until > ?12) AND (?10 IS NULL OR c.kind = 'direct' AND c.last_at <= ?12 - ?10 * 1000 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM messages lm WHERE lm.n = c.last_message AND lm.mine = 0 AND lm.kind NOT IN ('system', 'call'))) AND (?11 = 0 OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM favourites s WHERE s.chat = c.n) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM folder_chats fc JOIN favourites s ON s.folder = fc.folder WHERE fc.chat = c.n)) ORDER BY c.sort DESC, c.n DESC LIMIT ?4
    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_by_sort (archived=? AND sort<?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 12
      SEARCH lm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 13
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 14
      SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=?)
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)

    SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_sort (folder=? AND (sort,chat)>(?,?))
    SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)
      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 10

p.verified_name, m.n, m.at, m.mine, m.kind, m.status, coalesce(m.summary, m.text, ''), mp.name, mp.push_name, mp.verified_name, mj.jid, iif(c.name IS NULL AND c.kind <> 'direct', (SELECT group_concat(shown, ', ') FROM ( SELECT coalesce(gp.name, gp.push_name, gp.verified_name) AS shown FROM group_members g JOIN person_jids gj ON gj.n = g.member JOIN people gp ON gp.n = gj.person WHERE g.chat = c.n AND shown IS NOT NULL LIMIT 4)), NULL), m.edited_at, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), (SELECT json_group_array(folder) FROM (SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = c.n UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = c.person)), (SELECT slot FROM favourites WHERE chat = c.n), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.unread), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.mentions), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), c.ephemeral FROM chats c LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = c.person LEFT JOIN messages m ON m.n = iif(c.kind = 'community', coalesce((SELECT g.last_message FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id AND g.last_message IS NOT NULL ORDER BY g.last_at DESC LIMIT 1), c.last_message), c.last_message) LEFT JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE c.archived = 0 AND (c.sort, c.n) > (?2, ?3) AND (?5 = 0 OR c.unread > 0 OR c.marked_unread <> 0) AND (?6 = 0 OR c.mentions > 0) AND (?7 IS NULL OR iif(?7 = 'direct', c.kind = 'direct', iif(?7 = 'group', c.kind IN ('group', 'announcement'), c.kind = 'community' OR c.community IS NOT NULL))) AND (?8 IS NULL OR c.community = ?8 OR c.id = ?8) AND (?9 = 0 OR c.muted_until > ?12) AND (?10 IS NULL OR c.kind = 'direct' AND c.last_at <= ?12 - ?10 * 1000 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM messages lm WHERE lm.n = c.last_message AND lm.mine = 0 AND lm.kind NOT IN ('system', 'call'))) AND (?11 = 0 OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM favourites s WHERE s.chat = c.n) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM folder_chats fc JOIN favourites s ON s.folder = fc.folder WHERE fc.chat = c.n)) ORDER BY c.sort, c.n LIMIT ?4
    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_by_sort (archived=? AND sort>?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 12
      SEARCH lm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 13
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 14
      SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=?)
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)
      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9

g.chat = c.n AND shown IS NOT NULL LIMIT 4)), NULL), m.edited_at, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), (SELECT json_group_array(folder) FROM (SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = c.n UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = c.person)), (SELECT slot FROM favourites WHERE chat = c.n), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.unread), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.mentions), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), c.ephemeral FROM chats c INDEXED BY chats_mentioned LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = c.person LEFT JOIN messages m ON m.n = iif(c.kind = 'community', coalesce((SELECT g.last_message FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id AND g.last_message IS NOT NULL ORDER BY g.last_at DESC LIMIT 1), c.last_message), c.last_message) LEFT JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE c.archived = 0 AND c.mentions > 0 AND (c.sort, c.n) > (?2, ?3) AND (?5 = 0 OR c.unread > 0 OR c.marked_unread <> 0) AND (?6 = 0 OR c.mentions > 0) AND (?7 IS NULL OR iif(?7 = 'direct', c.kind = 'direct', iif(?7 = 'group', c.kind IN ('group', 'announcement'), c.kind = 'community' OR c.community IS NOT NULL))) AND (?8 IS NULL OR c.community = ?8 OR c.id = ?8) AND (?9 = 0 OR c.muted_until > ?12) AND (?10 IS NULL OR c.kind = 'direct' AND c.last_at <= ?12 - ?10 * 1000 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM messages lm WHERE lm.n = c.last_message AND lm.mine = 0 AND lm.kind NOT IN ('system', 'call'))) AND (?11 = 0 OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM favourites s WHERE s.chat = c.n) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM folder_chats fc JOIN favourites s ON s.folder = fc.folder WHERE fc.chat = c.n)) ORDER BY c.sort, c.n LIMIT ?4
    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_mentioned (sort>?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 12
      SEARCH lm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 13
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 14
      SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=?)
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4

      SCAN (subquery-3)

        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)

    CO-ROUTINE (subquery-4)

    SCAN k USING INDEX chats_known_communities
    SEARCH c EXISTS USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

    SCAN k USING INDEX chats_known_communities
    SEARCH c EXISTS USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

-- src/store/lists.rs

    SCAN k USING INDEX chats_known_communities
    SEARCH c EXISTS USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

-- src/store/lists.rs
SELECT count(*) FROM chats k INDEXED BY chats_known_communities WHERE k.kind = 'community' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM folder_chats f WHERE f.folder = ?1 AND f.chat = k.n) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM chats c JOIN folder_chats f ON f.folder = ?1 AND f.chat = c.n WHERE c.community = k.id)
    SCAN k USING INDEX chats_known_communities
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (folder=? AND chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      SEARCH c USING COVERING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
      SEARCH f USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=? AND folder=?)

    SCAN k USING INDEX chats_known_communities
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
        SEARCH lm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
        SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 3
        SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=?)
        SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?)
    SEARCH c EXISTS USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 5
      SEARCH lm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 6
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=?)
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?)

    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)
      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 10
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

SELECT c.n, c.id, c.v, c.sort, c.kind, c.name, c.person, c.avatar, c.unread, c.mentions, c.pinned, c.muted_until, c.archived, c.marked_unread, c.draft, c.community, p.name, p.push_name, p.verified_name, m.n, m.at, m.mine, m.kind, m.status, coalesce(m.summary, m.text, ''), mp.name, mp.push_name, mp.verified_name, mj.jid, iif(c.name IS NULL AND c.kind <> 'direct', (SELECT group_concat(shown, ', ') FROM ( SELECT coalesce(gp.name, gp.push_name, gp.verified_name) AS shown FROM group_members g JOIN person_jids gj ON gj.n = g.member JOIN people gp ON gp.n = gj.person WHERE g.chat = c.n AND shown IS NOT NULL LIMIT 4)), NULL), m.edited_at, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), (SELECT json_group_array(folder) FROM (SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = c.n UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = c.person)), (SELECT slot FROM favourites WHERE chat = c.n), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.unread), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.mentions), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), c.ephemeral FROM chats c LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = c.person LEFT JOIN messages m ON m.n = iif(c.kind = 'community', coalesce((SELECT g.last_message FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id AND g.last_message IS NOT NULL ORDER BY g.last_at DESC LIMIT 1), c.last_message), c.last_message) LEFT JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE c.community = ?1 AND c.archived = 0 ORDER BY c.last_at DESC LIMIT ?2
    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)
      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 10
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

    SCAN f
    SEARCH k USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?) LEFT-JOIN

SELECT p.n, max(p.v, p.roster_v), p.roster_sort, p.name, p.push_name, p.verified_name, p.about, p.presence, p.last_seen, p.followed, coalesce(p.avatar, (SELECT a.avatar FROM chats a WHERE a.person = p.n AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM chat_merges g WHERE g.gone = a.n) LIMIT 1)), (SELECT d.id FROM chats d WHERE d.person = p.n AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM chat_merges g WHERE g.gone = d.n) LIMIT 1), (SELECT j.jid FROM person_jids j WHERE j.person = p.n AND j.jid LIKE '%@s.whatsapp.net' LIMIT 1), (SELECT json_group_array(i.folder) FROM folder_items i WHERE i.person = p.n) FROM people p WHERE (p.name IS NOT NULL OR p.direct_chats > 0) AND (p.roster_sort, p.n) > (?1, ?2) AND p.roster_sort < ?3 AND p.n NOT IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?5)) ORDER BY p.roster_sort, p.n LIMIT ?4
    SEARCH p USING INDEX people_roster (roster_sort>? AND roster_sort<?)
    LIST SUBQUERY 7
      SCAN json_each VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
      CREATE BLOOM FILTER
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      SEARCH a USING INDEX chats_by_person (person=?)

      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 3

    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 5
      SEARCH j USING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 6
      SEARCH i USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)

    SEARCH p USING INDEX people_roster (roster_sort<?)
    SEARCH c EXISTS USING INDEX chats_by_person (person=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      SEARCH lm USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      SEARCH fc USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_chat (chat=?)
      SEARCH s USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_1 (folder=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      SEARCH a USING INDEX chats_by_person (person=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
        SEARCH g USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4

      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 3

    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 5
      SEARCH j USING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 6
      SEARCH i USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)

    SEARCH p USING INDEX people_roster (roster_sort<?)

      CREATE BLOOM FILTER

-- src/store/lists.rs

    SEARCH folder_people USING COVERING INDEX folder_people_by_sort (folder=? AND roster_sort<?)

-- src/store/lists.rs
SELECT count(*) FROM folders f JOIN folder_counts k ON k.folder = f.n WHERE f.builtin IS NOT NULL AND (k.chats, k.unread, k.mentions) IS NOT (SELECT count(c.n), coalesce(sum(c.unread), 0), coalesce(sum(c.mentions), 0) FROM chats c WHERE CASE f.builtin WHEN 'all' THEN c.archived = 0 WHEN 'unread' THEN c.archived = 0 AND (c.unread > 0 OR c.marked_unread <> 0) WHEN 'groups' THEN c.archived = 0 AND c.kind IN ('group', 'announcement') WHEN 'communities' THEN c.archived = 0 AND (c.kind = 'community' OR c.community IS NOT NULL) ELSE c.archived <> 0 END)
    SEARCH f USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_folders_1 (builtin>?)
    SEARCH k USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SCAN c

-- src/store/lists.rs
WITH RECURSIVE up (folder, ancestor) AS ( SELECT n, n FROM folders WHERE builtin IS NULL AND rule IS NULL UNION SELECT up.folder, f.parent FROM up JOIN folders f ON f.n = up.ancestor WHERE f.parent IS NOT NULL) SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT folder, ancestor FROM up EXCEPT SELECT folder, ancestor FROM folder_paths)) + (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT folder, ancestor FROM folder_paths EXCEPT SELECT folder, ancestor FROM up))
    SCAN CONSTANT ROW
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 5
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-4)
        MERGE (EXCEPT)
          LEFT
            MATERIALIZE up
              SETUP
                SEARCH folders USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_folders_1 (builtin=?)
              RECURSIVE STEP
                SCAN up
                SEARCH f USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
            SCAN up
            USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
          RIGHT
            SCAN folder_paths
      SCAN (subquery-4)
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-7)
        MERGE (EXCEPT)
          LEFT
            SCAN folder_paths
          RIGHT
            SCAN up
            USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
      SCAN (subquery-7)

-- src/store/lists.rs
WITH reach (folder, chat) AS ( SELECT p.ancestor, i.chat FROM folder_items i JOIN folder_paths p ON p.folder = i.folder WHERE i.chat IS NOT NULL UNION ALL SELECT p.ancestor, c.n FROM folder_items i JOIN folder_paths p ON p.folder = i.folder JOIN chats c ON c.person = i.person WHERE i.person IS NOT NULL), counted (folder, chat, paths) AS (SELECT folder, chat, count(*) FROM reach GROUP BY folder, chat) SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT folder, chat, paths FROM counted EXCEPT SELECT folder, chat, paths FROM folder_chats)) + (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT folder, chat, paths FROM folder_chats EXCEPT SELECT folder, chat, paths FROM counted))
    SCAN CONSTANT ROW
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 6
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-5)
        MERGE (EXCEPT)
          LEFT
            MATERIALIZE counted
              MATERIALIZE reach
                COMPOUND QUERY
                  LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
                    SCAN i USING COVERING INDEX folder_items_chats
                    SEARCH p USING PRIMARY KEY (folder=?)
                  UNION ALL
                    SCAN i USING COVERING INDEX folder_items_people
                    SEARCH p USING PRIMARY KEY (folder=?)
                    SEARCH c USING COVERING INDEX chats_by_person (person=?)
              SCAN reach
              USE TEMP B-TREE FOR GROUP BY
            SCAN counted
            USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
          RIGHT
            SCAN folder_chats
      SCAN (subquery-5)
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-8)
        MERGE (EXCEPT)
          LEFT
            SCAN folder_chats
          RIGHT
            SCAN counted
            USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
      SCAN (subquery-8)

-- src/store/lists.rs
WITH counted (folder, person, paths) AS ( SELECT p.ancestor, i.person, count(*) FROM folder_items i JOIN folder_paths p ON p.folder = i.folder WHERE i.person IS NOT NULL GROUP BY p.ancestor, i.person) SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT folder, person, paths FROM counted EXCEPT SELECT folder, person, paths FROM folder_people)) + (SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT folder, person, paths FROM folder_people EXCEPT SELECT folder, person, paths FROM counted))
    SCAN CONSTANT ROW
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        MERGE (EXCEPT)
          LEFT
            MATERIALIZE counted
              SCAN p USING COVERING INDEX folder_paths_by_ancestor
              SEARCH i USING COVERING INDEX folder_items_people (folder=? AND person>?)
              USE TEMP B-TREE FOR GROUP BY
            SCAN counted
            USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
          RIGHT
            SCAN folder_people
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        MERGE (EXCEPT)
          LEFT
            SCAN folder_people
          RIGHT
            SCAN counted
            USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
      SCAN (subquery-6)

-- src/store/lists.rs
SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM folder_chats f JOIN chats c ON c.n = f.chat WHERE f.sort IS NOT c.sort OR f.unread IS NOT c.unread OR f.mentions IS NOT c.mentions) + (SELECT count(*) FROM folder_people f JOIN people p ON p.n = f.person WHERE f.roster_sort IS NOT p.roster_sort OR f.lone IS NOT (p.direct_chats = 0))
    SCAN CONSTANT ROW
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SCAN f
      SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      SCAN f
      SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/store/lists.rs
SELECT count(*) FROM people p WHERE p.direct_chats IS NOT (SELECT count(*) FROM chats c WHERE c.person = p.n)
    SCAN p
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH c USING COVERING INDEX chats_by_person (person=?)

-- src/store/lists.rs
SELECT count(*) FROM folders f JOIN folder_counts k ON k.folder = f.n WHERE f.builtin IS NULL AND f.rule IS NULL AND (k.chats, k.unread, k.mentions, k.lone) IS NOT ( SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM folder_chats WHERE folder = f.n), (SELECT coalesce(sum(unread), 0) FROM folder_chats WHERE folder = f.n), (SELECT coalesce(sum(mentions), 0) FROM folder_chats WHERE folder = f.n), (SELECT count(*) FROM folder_people WHERE folder = f.n AND lone))
    SEARCH f USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_folders_1 (builtin=?)
    SEARCH k USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 5
      SCAN CONSTANT ROW
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
        SEARCH folder_chats USING COVERING INDEX folder_chats_by_sort (folder=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
        SEARCH folder_chats USING PRIMARY KEY (folder=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 3
        SEARCH folder_chats USING PRIMARY KEY (folder=?)
      CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
        SEARCH folder_people USING PRIMARY KEY (folder=?)

-- src/store/lists.rs
SELECT count(*) FROM folders f WHERE (f.builtin IS NOT NULL OR f.rule IS NULL) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM folder_counts WHERE folder = f.n)
    SCAN f
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH folder_counts USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

    SEARCH people USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- src/store/read.rs
SELECT c.n, c.id, c.v, c.sort, c.kind, c.name, c.person, c.avatar, c.unread, c.mentions, c.pinned, c.muted_until, c.archived, c.marked_unread, c.draft, c.community, p.name, p.push_name, p.verified_name, m.n, m.at, m.mine, m.kind, m.status, coalesce(m.summary, m.text, ''), mp.name, mp.push_name, mp.verified_name, mj.jid, iif(c.name IS NULL AND c.kind <> 'direct', (SELECT group_concat(shown, ', ') FROM ( SELECT coalesce(gp.name, gp.push_name, gp.verified_name) AS shown FROM group_members g JOIN person_jids gj ON gj.n = g.member JOIN people gp ON gp.n = gj.person WHERE g.chat = c.n AND shown IS NOT NULL LIMIT 4)), NULL), m.edited_at, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), (SELECT json_group_array(folder) FROM (SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = c.n UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = c.person)), (SELECT slot FROM favourites WHERE chat = c.n), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.unread), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.mentions), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), c.ephemeral FROM chats c LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = c.person LEFT JOIN messages m ON m.n = iif(c.kind = 'community', coalesce((SELECT g.last_message FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id AND g.last_message IS NOT NULL ORDER BY g.last_at DESC LIMIT 1), c.last_message), c.last_message) LEFT JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE c.archived = ?1 AND (c.sort, c.n) < (?2, ?3) ORDER BY c.sort DESC, c.n DESC LIMIT ?4
    SEARCH c USING INDEX chats_by_sort (archived=? AND sort<?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-6)
        COMPOUND QUERY
          LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL
            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_person (person=?)
      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 10
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)

SELECT c.n, c.id, c.v, c.sort, c.kind, c.name, c.person, c.avatar, c.unread, c.mentions, c.pinned, c.muted_until, c.archived, c.marked_unread, c.draft, c.community, p.name, p.push_name, p.verified_name, m.n, m.at, m.mine, m.kind, m.status, coalesce(m.summary, m.text, ''), mp.name, mp.push_name, mp.verified_name, mj.jid, iif(c.name IS NULL AND c.kind <> 'direct', (SELECT group_concat(shown, ', ') FROM ( SELECT coalesce(gp.name, gp.push_name, gp.verified_name) AS shown FROM group_members g JOIN person_jids gj ON gj.n = g.member JOIN people gp ON gp.n = gj.person WHERE g.chat = c.n AND shown IS NOT NULL LIMIT 4)), NULL), m.edited_at, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), (SELECT json_group_array(folder) FROM (SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE chat = c.n UNION ALL SELECT folder FROM folder_items WHERE person = c.person)), (SELECT slot FROM favourites WHERE chat = c.n), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.unread), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), iif(c.kind = 'community', (SELECT coalesce(sum(g.mentions), 0) FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id), NULL), c.ephemeral FROM chats c LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = c.person LEFT JOIN messages m ON m.n = iif(c.kind = 'community', coalesce((SELECT g.last_message FROM chats g WHERE g.community = c.id AND g.last_message IS NOT NULL ORDER BY g.last_at DESC LIMIT 1), c.last_message), c.last_message) LEFT JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE c.n = ?1
    SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH g USING INDEX chats_by_community (community=?)
    SEARCH mj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 2
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-1)
        SEARCH g USING PRIMARY KEY (chat=?)
        SEARCH gj USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH gp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN (subquery-1)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-3)
        SEARCH outbox USING INDEX outbox_by_target (target=?)
      SCAN (subquery-3)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7

            SEARCH folder_items USING INDEX folder_items_by_chat (chat=?)
          UNION ALL

      SCAN (subquery-6)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 8
      SEARCH favourites USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_favourites_2 (chat=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 9

    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 10

json_set(m.reply, '$.from', coalesce( iif(instr(' ' || coalesce((SELECT value FROM sync_state WHERE key = 'me'), '') || ' ', ' ' || (m.reply ->> 'from') || ' ') > 0, 'You', NULL), rp.name, rp.push_name, rp.verified_name, m.reply ->> 'from'))), m.detail, m.forwarded, m.edited_at, m.deleted, m.expires_at, m.starred, m.view_once, p.n, p.name, p.push_name, p.verified_name, j.jid, (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('emoji', emoji, 'n', n, 'mine', json(iif(mine, 'true', 'false')))) FROM (SELECT emoji, count(*) AS n, max(mine) AS mine, min(at) AS first FROM reactions WHERE message = m.n GROUP BY emoji ORDER BY first)), iif(instr(m.spans, '"t":"mention"') > 0, (SELECT json_object('names', json_group_object(e.value ->> 'person', coalesce(mp.name, mp.push_name, mp.verified_name)), 'v', max(mp.v)) FROM json_each(m.spans) e JOIN person_jids mj ON mj.jid = e.value ->> 'person' JOIN people mp ON mp.n = mj.person WHERE e.value ->> 't' = 'mention'), NULL), (SELECT json_group_array(json_object('at', created_at, 'effect', json(effect), 'mine_at', (SELECT max(r.at) FROM reactions r WHERE r.message = m.n AND r.mine))) FROM (SELECT created_at, effect FROM outbox WHERE target = m.n AND effect IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'failed' ORDER BY n)), iif(instr(m.detail, '"thumbnail":"') > 0, (SELECT h.sha256 FROM thumbnails h WHERE h.sha256 = m.detail ->> '$.link.thumbnail'), NULL), m.reply ->> 'from', (SELECT json_object('state', iif(f.path IS NOT NULL, 'local', md.state), 'progress', md.progress, 'error', md.error, 'path', f.path, 'small', f.small, 'large', f.large, 'thumbnail', f.thumbnail, 'has_thumbnail', md.thumbnail, 'sha256', hex(md.sha256), 'hd', (SELECT json_object('path', hf.path) FROM media h LEFT JOIN media_files hf ON hf.sha256 = h.sha256 WHERE h.message = m.n AND h.variant = 'hd')) FROM media md LEFT JOIN media_files f ON f.sha256 = md.sha256 WHERE md.message = m.n AND md.variant = 'sd') FROM messages m JOIN chats c ON c.n = m.chat LEFT JOIN person_jids j ON j.n = m.sender LEFT JOIN people p ON p.n = j.person LEFT JOIN person_jids rj ON rj.jid = m.reply ->> 'from' LEFT JOIN people rp ON rp.n = rj.person WHERE m.chat = (SELECT n FROM chats WHERE id = ?1) ORDER BY m.at DESC, m.n DESC LIMIT ?2
    SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
      SEARCH chats USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_chats_1 (id=?)
    REUSE SUBQUERY 11
    SEARCH m USING INDEX messages_by_time (chat=?)
    SEARCH j USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH p USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH rj USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_person_jids_1 (jid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SEARCH rp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?) LEFT-JOIN
    SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH sync_state USING PRIMARY KEY (key=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 3
      CO-ROUTINE (subquery-2)
        SEARCH reactions USING PRIMARY KEY (message=?)
        USE TEMP B-TREE FOR GROUP BY
        USE TEMP B-TREE FOR ORDER BY
      SCAN (subquery-2)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      SCAN e VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
      SEARCH mj USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_person_jids_1 (jid=?)
      SEARCH mp USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7

      SEARCH h USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?)

      SEARCH f USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

        SEARCH hf USING PRIMARY KEY (sha256=?) LEFT-JOIN

-- src/store/write/events.rs
SELECT u.message FROM unopened u JOIN messages m ON m.n = u.message JOIN chats c ON c.n = m.chat WHERE m.sender IN (SELECT n FROM person_jids WHERE person = ?1) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pending p WHERE p.chat = c.id AND p.target = m.id AND p.kind IN ('rsvp', 'secret') AND p.sender IN (SELECT jid FROM person_jids WHERE person = ?1)) OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM person_jids j JOIN quarantine q ON q.source IN ('answer', 'edit') AND q.ref >= c.id || ' ' || m.id || ' ' || j.jid || ' ' AND q.ref < c.id || ' ' || m.id || ' ' || j.jid || '!' WHERE j.person = ?1)
    SCAN u
    SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SEARCH c USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    LIST SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH person_jids USING COVERING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)
      CREATE BLOOM FILTER
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 3
      SEARCH p USING INDEX pending_by_target_at (chat=? AND target=?)
      LIST SUBQUERY 2
        SEARCH person_jids USING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)
        CREATE BLOOM FILTER
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 4
      SEARCH j USING INDEX person_jids_by_person (person=?)
      SEARCH q USING COVERING INDEX quarantine_by_ref (source=? AND ref>? AND ref<?)

    SEARCH quarantine USING COVERING INDEX quarantine_by_ref (source=? AND ref>? AND ref<?)

-- src/store/write/folders.rs
WITH RECURSIVE up (folder, ancestor) AS ( SELECT n, n FROM folders WHERE builtin IS NULL AND rule IS NULL UNION SELECT up.folder, f.parent FROM up JOIN folders f ON f.n = up.ancestor WHERE f.parent IS NOT NULL) INSERT INTO folder_paths (folder, ancestor) SELECT folder, ancestor FROM up
    CO-ROUTINE up
      SETUP
        SEARCH folders USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_folders_1 (builtin=?)
      RECURSIVE STEP
        SCAN up
        SEARCH f USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    SCAN up

-- src/store/write/folders.rs
UPDATE folder_counts SET (chats, unread, mentions) = (SELECT count(c.n), coalesce(sum(c.unread), 0), coalesce(sum(c.mentions), 0) FROM folders f, chats c WHERE f.n = folder_counts.folder AND CASE f.builtin WHEN 'all' THEN c.archived = 0 WHEN 'unread' THEN c.archived = 0 AND (c.unread > 0 OR c.marked_unread <> 0) WHEN 'groups' THEN c.archived = 0 AND c.kind IN ('group', 'announcement') WHEN 'communities' THEN c.archived = 0 AND (c.kind = 'community' OR c.community IS NOT NULL) ELSE c.archived <> 0 END) WHERE folder IN (SELECT n FROM folders WHERE builtin IS NOT NULL)
    SEARCH folder_counts USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
    LIST SUBQUERY 2
      SEARCH folders USING COVERING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_folders_1 (builtin>?)
    CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 1
      SEARCH f USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      SCAN c

    SEARCH chats USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)

-- trigger links_derived_insert
INSERT INTO links (message, place, chat, at, sender, mine, url, host, title, description) SELECT message, place, chat, at, sender, mine, url, host, title, description FROM message_links WHERE message = ?
    COMPOUND QUERY
      LEFT-MOST SUBQUERY
        SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SCAN e VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 15
          SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
          SCAN e VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        SCAN r VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        SCAN a VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        SCAN h VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 11
          USE TEMP B-TREE FOR count(DISTINCT)
          SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
          SCAN e VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 13
          SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 13
          SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
      UNION ALL
        SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 7
          SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
          SCAN e VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
        SCAN r VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        SCAN a VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        SCAN h VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
        CORRELATED SCALAR SUBQUERY 5
          USE TEMP B-TREE FOR count(DISTINCT)
          SEARCH m USING INTEGER PRIMARY KEY (rowid=?)
          SCAN e VIRTUAL TABLE INDEX 1:
